============================================================
3. BOOK-READINESS PROBE (real small caps + oldstyle figures)
============================================================
One upright face probed per family (probing every file would take far
longer and say the same thing).

  smcp = real small capitals      onum = oldstyle (text) figures

Both matter here. The book is full of datelines and numbers, and our
foundation currently fakes small caps by letterspacing uppercase.

============================================================
4. TARGETED CHECK — the families actually under consideration
============================================================
For each: is it in the TeX tree, and does it have the four styles our
\setmainfont pattern needs? A READY block can be pasted straight into
donna-book-foundation.sty.

The first group is what the Aug-2025 fontconfig capture said this
machine has; the second is worth checking even though that capture
did not list it, since the capture also missed IBM Plex.

======== group A: listed in the Aug-2025 capture ========

### EB Garamond — first choice. The capture shows dedicated SC (small-caps) faces, which is exactly what our faux-small-caps workaround exists to replace.
    /usr/share/texlive/texmf-dist/fonts/opentype/public/ebgaramond/EBGaramond-BoldItalic.otf
    /usr/share/texlive/texmf-dist/fonts/opentype/public/ebgaramond/EBGaramond-Bold.otf
    /usr/share/texlive/texmf-dist/fonts/opentype/public/ebgaramond/EBGaramond-ExtraBoldItalic.otf
    /usr/share/texlive/texmf-dist/fonts/opentype/public/ebgaramond/EBGaramond-ExtraBold.otf
    /usr/share/texlive/texmf-dist/fonts/opentype/public/ebgaramond/EBGaramond-Italic.otf
    /usr/share/texlive/texmf-dist/fonts/opentype/public/ebgaramond/EBGaramond-MediumItalic.otf
    /usr/share/texlive/texmf-dist/fonts/opentype/public/ebgaramond/EBGaramond-Medium.otf
    /usr/share/texlive/texmf-dist/fonts/opentype/public/ebgaramond/EBGaramond-Regular.otf
    /usr/share/texlive/texmf-dist/fonts/opentype/public/ebgaramond/EBGaramond-SemiBoldItalic.otf
    /usr/share/texlive/texmf-dist/fonts/opentype/public/ebgaramond/EBGaramond-SemiBold.otf

    READY — all four styles present:
      \setmainfont{EBGaramond}[Extension=.otf,
        UprightFont=*-Regular, BoldFont=*-Bold,
        ItalicFont=*-Italic,   BoldItalicFont=*-BoldItalic]
      real small caps: YES     oldstyle figures: YES
    /usr/share/texlive/texmf-dist/fonts/opentype/public/ebgaramond/EBGaramond-BoldItalic.otf
    /usr/share/texlive/texmf-dist/fonts/opentype/public/ebgaramond/EBGaramond-Bold.otf
    /usr/share/texlive/texmf-dist/fonts/opentype/public/ebgaramond/EBGaramond-ExtraBoldItalic.otf
    /usr/share/texlive/texmf-dist/fonts/opentype/public/ebgaramond/EBGaramond-ExtraBold.otf
    /usr/share/texlive/texmf-dist/fonts/opentype/public/ebgaramond/EBGaramond-Italic.otf
    /usr/share/texlive/texmf-dist/fonts/opentype/public/ebgaramond/EBGaramond-MediumItalic.otf

============================================================
5. FONTCONFIG VIEW (system fonts, by NAME)

Our foundation file records that fontconfig here could not resolve most
fonts by name, which is why we load by filename. This section says
whether that is still true, and lets us diff against the Aug-2025 list.
